# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-09-04)

## Corpus Check
- 1784 files · ~7,355,552 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 37043 nodes · 91774 edges · 1729 communities (918 shown, 811 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2292 edges (avg confidence: 0.66)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `c00592d5`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
